Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the block structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 534. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 914 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_534(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 534. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^914(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)
